Model before rewriting:

letting a be {2,3}
find b: set (size 2) of int(1..3)

such that

b = a

b
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
b
new variables:
  find b#packed_1: int(0..7)
new constraints:
  sum([(b#packed_1 / 1) % 2,(b#packed_1 / 2) % 2,(b#packed_1 / 4) % 2;int(1..)]) = 2

--

b#packed_1
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
b#packed_1
new variables:
  find b#packed_1#offset_1: bool
  find b#packed_1#offset_2: bool
  find b#packed_1#offset_3: bool
new constraints:
  or([!(b#packed_1#offset_3),or([!(b#packed_1#offset_2),or([!(b#packed_1#offset_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)])
  or([and([SATInt(Offset, [b#packed_1#offset_1,b#packed_1#offset_2,b#packed_1#offset_3;int(1..)] [0, 7]) >= 0,SATInt(Offset, [b#packed_1#offset_1,b#packed_1#offset_2,b#packed_1#offset_3;int(1..)] [0, 7]) <= 7;int(1..)]);int(1..)])

   ~~> select_integer_relation ([("SAT", 18500)])
